Credo landing pages: QA report

Updated 24 Sept 2026: the Webflow source audit and the prototype-vs-Webflow comparison are merged in at the top; the 23 Sept cross-browser results follow below, with 24 Sept notes on the findings whose cause is now known.
Audit: prototype vs Webflow (22–24 Sept)Cross-browser check (23 Sept)

Audit: prototype vs Webflow, 22–24 Sept

Verdict. The build is faithful: compared against what the prototypes actually render, the live pages carry the same headline, sub-headline, sections, cards, rights items and FAQs. What is broken sits in three places: pages carrying another vertical's content (four wholesale, two in their rights section), the page head on all 30 (canonical, descriptions, heading semantics, schema), and firm details that contradict the 21 Sep decisions (entity, address).

Correction, same day. An earlier version of this audit reported "copy fidelity 79–87%" and a missing hero eyebrow on every page. Both were artefacts of comparing the live pages against the content model (content-*.js) rather than the rendered prototype. The eyebrow is switched off by design in this variant ([data-labels="plain"] .hero-no { display: none }), and the apparent copy gaps were mostly list numbering and internal labels that never render. Re-checked against rendered output, body copy matches; the genuine differences are listed per page below.

Prototype ↔ live mapping: 26 of 30 live pages map to a content-*.js model. Four live slugs have no prototype (collection-defense, credit-cards, fcba-and-fdcpa, stop-wage-garnishment) — and all four are the broken ones. Seven prototypes have no live page: content.js (champion), content-act-fast, content-more-money, content-payday-fight-back, content-payday-lawsuit-proof, content-payday-lawsuit-respond, content-payday-rights.

General — applies to all 30 pages unless noted

#IssueEvidenceRecommended fixPriority
G1Canonical on every page points to https://start.credolegal.com/ — the homepage, so all 30 pages declare themselves to be the same page and the homepage absorbs the ranking credit. og:url the same, so every share previews as the homepage.30/30Point each page's canonical and og:url at that page's own full production URL (https://start.credolegal.com/<slug>) — not the homepage, and not the enroll address, which is staging only.Critical
G2The old domain still serves the same 30 slugs with near-identical content.start.credolegal.com/debt-harassment-stop-calls etc. return 200Pick the primary domain, 301 the other slug-for-slug. Until then G1 is actively de-indexing enroll.Critical
G3Split per page (22 Sep). No <meta name="description"> on any of the 30. Now carried as one comment per page, each quoting that page's prototype description.30/30Use the prototype's own <meta name="description"> text for all three: meta description, og:description, twitter:description.High
G4No og:title / og:description (og:image present).30/30Add per page; social/chat shares currently show the raw title only.Medium
G5Heading semantics: 30 <h1> per page, zero <h2>. Every section label and form question is an H1.30/30 (h3 ×4, h4 ×25–28)One H1 (the hero); sections → H2; cards → H3.High
G6Schema LegalService points url at start.credolegal.com and pulls logo/image from a different Webflow project id. No FAQPage schema despite 5–6 Q&As per page.30/30Correct url/logo; add FAQPage.Medium
G7Split per page (22 Sep). Titles: 18/30 exceed 60 characters; 16/30 open with "Let's Take The First Step To…"; two pages share one title. Now carried as one comment per page, each naming that page's own headline.see per-pageHouse rule, applied per page: page title = {H1} | Credo Legal; og:title = the H1; twitter:title = the H1 — with any full stop removed from the H1 in all three.High
G8Footer NAP says "Credo Legal Services, P.A." and "1 Liberty Plaza, Suite 401" — contradicts the 21 Sep decision (1 Liberty Street, Suite 4010) and the P.C. entity used on Nextdoor/Trustpilot. BBB seal alt text also says P.A.30/30Align entity + address everywhere, including the BBB image alt.High (compliance)
G9The lead form posts to formspree.io/f/xzzyzzwj — a third-party relay receiving name, phone, email, address and debt details. Cloudflare Turnstile is in place.30/30Confirm routing into the CRM and a DPA covering the PII; move to a first-party endpoint if none.Critical (privacy)
G10No TCPA/SMS consent line and no "not legal advice" near the form; privacy policy is footer-only.30/30Add consent copy under the submit button (the form asks for two phone numbers).High (compliance)
G11Trustpilot widget renders empty — iframe present, 150px tall, no visible content, desktop and mobile.9/9 renderedVerify business unit 66f1b872822deaf8e3b0c570 has published reviews and the template is right; otherwise remove the dead space.Medium
G12Hero and section imagery is CSS background-image: no alt text, not preloadable for LCP, and the 960px asset is served to 375px mobile. The prototype ships <picture> with 480/960 webp, loading="eager", alt text, and mobile "tight" crops.30/30Move the hero to <picture>/<img> with responsive sources and alt; keep decorative section art as background.Medium
G13Withdrawn. Reported as a missing hero eyebrow; the eyebrow is hidden by design in this prototype variant, so the live pages match.—None.—
G14Withdrawn 22 Sep. The tracked numbers were checked against the campaign phone-number sheet with UTM-tagged test links: all 47 page/source pairs match. The hub phone map this row compared against was stale.—None.—
G15No GA4 tag in source — only GTM container GTM-PTLMPVGH (Microsoft UET does fire).30/30Confirm GA4 + conversion tags fire from inside GTM; cannot be confirmed from source.High (verify)
G16Six different CTA labels on one page: "FREE REVIEW →", "GET A FREE CASE EVALUATION →", "Get a free case evaluation →", "SEE IF YOU QUALIFY, FREE →", "SPEAK TO AN ATTORNEY NOW →", "continue →".30/30Standardise to two (primary form CTA + call CTA) and one casing rule.Medium
G17Form parity with the prototype: debt-count buckets differ (live 1-2 / 3-5 / 6+ vs proto 1 / 2–3 / 4–5 / 6+), security options reworded and reordered, 12 debt-type checkboxes vs the prototype's shorter list, and the details step diverges — live adds a required Alternative-Phone-Number and an address field, and leaves zip-code optional where the prototype requires a validated 5-digit ZIP. Corrected 22 Sep: an earlier version of this row claimed the prototype's city and date-of-birth fields were missing live. They are absent from the rendered prototype too — detailLabels still carries leftover labels for altPhone/address/city/dob, but StepDetails() renders only first, last, phone, email, state, zip. UTM/gclid/gbraid/wbraid capture is present and correct.30/30Prototype is canonical (decided 22 Sep): match it — count buckets 1 / 2–3 / 4–5 / 6+, its security wording and order, its shorter debt-type list; drop Alternative Phone and Address, make ZIP a required 5-digit field.Medium

Exemptions: none — G1–G17 apply to all 30 pages. The statute sidepane from the prototype pilot is absent everywhere, which is expected: it was a single-LP pilot, not part of this build.

Checked and clean: robots index, follow, lang="en", favicon, og:image, Turnstile, one form per page, UTM and gclid/gbraid/wbraid capture (fbclid is not captured — see G20), "Attorney Advertising" label, "Prior results do not guarantee a similar outcome", the state-exclusion line, 0 images missing alt, no horizontal overflow at 375px, all 30 URLs return 200.

Status at the source — 24 Sep

Checked directly in Webflow through the official MCP connector (read-only). The staging site is NEW Credo Legal Landing Pages (6a5be784cc1cab7ef8e99233, enroll.credolegal.com). Production is a separate site, Credo Legal Landing Pages (6903ad8d7b4674d0123baecd, start.credolegal.com). What was read: page settings, page and site custom code, registered scripts, form settings, the element tree and styles. The developer published staging at 12:55 UTC on 24 Sep, after the last page edits (10:37–10:55 UTC), so the served pages match the Webflow source. Every behaviour item was also checked in a browser.

The 26 pages with a prototype carry identical custom code apart from the slug, so each result applies to all 26 unless noted. The four pages without a prototype were checked separately.

#Status 24 SepWhat the source shows
Wrong content (4 pages)Opencollection-defense, credit-cards, fcba-and-fdcpa and stop-wage-garnishment still show the payday body (H1 "Payday Lender Suing You?", 20 payday mentions each). Their canonicals now point at their own URLs.
G1FixedEach page's head code sets canonical and og:url to https://start.credolegal.com/<slug>. The served HTML carries exactly one canonical tag.
G2OpenBoth Webflow sites are live and serve the same slugs. Production was last published 23 Sep, and most of its pages were last edited 4 Jun.
G3FixedMeta description, og:description and twitter:description all equal the prototype's own meta description on 26/26. They run 162–249 characters because the prototype texts are that long.
G4Fixedog:title and og:description are set per page in Webflow's page settings.
G5FixedEvery page has one H1, 18 H2s, 35–37 H3s and 5–7 H4s. In each section the small label and the headline are both H2. The rights items are H3/H4 pairs.
G6OpenWebflow's own schema field is empty on all 26. The JSON-LD sits in each page's head custom code: a LegalService whose url is the homepage, whose logo/image come from the production site's asset store, and whose address is 2151 W Hillsboro Blvd, Deerfield Beach, FL 33442. There is no FAQPage, and twitter:site is the placeholder @handle.

G17Mostly fixedAlternative Phone and Address are gone; ZIP is required (5 digits, pattern-checked); the six debt types match the prototype. The count and security dropdowns show the prototype's options in the browser, but Webflow still stores the old ones (1-2 / 3-5 / 6+; "Secured with collateral / Unsecured / Don't Know"). A site-wide head script swaps them in on load. The debt-stage options differ from the prototype in capitalisation and wording ("Behind On Payments", "Being Sued/Served Papers"). The phone input is type="text" in Webflow's settings.

G3, follow-up 24 Sep. The descriptions now equal the prototype's text, as G3 asked. That is why all 26 run 162–249 characters and fail the ≤160 check in the 23 Sep cross-browser report. Decision (24 Sep): the descriptions will be shortened to ≤160 characters, first in the prototypes and then copied to Webflow. The new text per page is listed under "Shortened meta descriptions" below.

Where the cross-browser findings (23 Sep) come from

  • multi-step.js is loaded from each page's footer code. It looks for form[data-form="multistep"], and no element on these pages has a data-form attribute. That's why it throws "multistepForm is null". The steps, validation and submit all run from the site-wide head script instead. The POST to videsigns-staging.co.uk/counter comes from inside multi-step.js itself (line 2500 of the library), not from Credo's code.
  • The phone swap is an Embed element at the top of each page body, not custom code.
  • The "or call" contrast failure is the style callustext Copy (.callustext-copy, colour #c8cdd6) on the white form card. The other #c8cdd6 text (.callnumbers, .callustext) sits on the dark #111418 footer and CTA, where it passes.

Added 24 Sep — full code review and prototype-vs-Webflow comparison

Method: all 26 pages and their published prototypes were rendered in Chromium at 1440×900 and 390×844 (iPhone-size, touch). Visible text, computed styles, images and section layout were extracted from both and diffed; the reference page (debt-lawsuit-attorney) was also compared side by side in screenshots. All site and page custom code, registered scripts, form settings and styles were read in Webflow. Behaviour items were tested in the browser. The footer is excluded: it follows the separate footer handoff, not the prototype.

#IssueEvidenceRecommended fixPriority
G18Statute citations change case. Every citation label style (.mjfdcpaboxtext, -copy, -copy-copy, -copy-copy-copy) has text-transform: uppercase, so subsection letters render as capitals: "FDCPA § 1692e(2)" shows as "§ 1692E(2)", "§ 1692c(a)(1)" as "§ 1692C(A)(1)", "FRCP 60(b)" as "60(B)". The prototype shows them in their correct case.32 citations on 18/26 pages (source text is correct; only the display changes)Remove uppercase from the citation label styles and type any word labels ("Civil right", "Due process") in the case the prototype shows.High
G19On mobile, the sticky call bar covers the form's Continue button. The fixed bar .sticky-call-button ("Is your debt situation becoming urgent? Call Now", 72px tall) sits at the bottom of the screen. At 390×844 it lies exactly over the Continue button on first view: a tap there hits the bar, not the button. The prototype has no sticky bar.26/26, tested at 390×844Keep the primary form CTA clear: hide the bar while the form is in view, or remove it.High
G20The Meta click ID is never captured. The UTM script in the site head stores fbclig (typo) instead of fbclid, and the form has no fbclid field. Test with ?fbclid=…&gclid=…: gclid landed in a cookie and a form field, fbclid landed nowhere. Meta leads therefore reach formspree without their click ID. (The Meta pixel still sets its own _fbc cookie.)26/26Correct the key to fbclid and add a hidden fbclid field, the same way gclid is carried.High
G21Call-click tracking — needs discussion. (a) The site-head tracker that posts call clicks to credo.debtfixer.co/getUTM.php listens only for ids clicktocall, clicktocall1 and clicktocall2, and none of them exist. The call links are #navcta, #herocta, #footercta and #bfootercta, so it never fires. (b) GA4 does see call clicks through GTM, but each is an event named after the link text or number. Since 17 Sep on enroll: "IS YOUR DEBT SITUATION BECOMING URGENT?" 17, "(347) 523-4568" 3, "(720) 414-1751" 2. None is a key event. The only phone key events are two old numbers, (212) 918-0095 and (212) 920-1247.26/26; GA4 property 474656297To discuss: point the tracker at the real call-link ids or remove it; send one consistent GA4 event (e.g. call_click with the number as a parameter) and mark it as a key event.Medium — needs discussion
G22Intro paragraph in Title Case. The "What we do" paragraph style .text-block-16 has text-transform: capitalize, so it shows as "At Credo Legal, We Level The Playing Field. We Review The Claim…". The prototype renders normal sentence case.25/26Remove capitalize from .text-block-16.Medium
G23The H1 accent word isn't red. Each prototype H1 has one red accent word ("Is a Creditor Suing You?"). The prototype builds it as prefix<em>accent</em>suffix from the page's h1 array, styled by one rule (.h1 em, .h2 em { color: var(--credo-red); font-style: normal }). Live H1s are plain text with no accent. The bottom CTA does have a native <em>decision</em>, but a page-footer script also regex-searches #ctaheading for "decision" and wraps it in an inline color:#ff6b73 span. That script is redundant, depends on the keyword, runs after load (so the colour flashes in) and uses a different red; the <em> also shows italic.26/26Put the accent word in each H1 as a native inline element (as the bottom CTA already does), styled by one global class (e.g. accent: #c92028, font-style: normal) and used for H1 and bottom CTA alike. Delete the regex script. Accent words per page are in each content-*.js hero.h1 (the middle item).Medium
G24The mobile type scale is much larger than the prototype's. At 390px the H1 is 52px (prototype 32px) and section headlines are 42px (prototype 26px), so headlines wrap to 3–4 lines and push the form down. Some wrapped headings also have oversized line gaps (e.g. "How much do you currently owe in / total?").26/26Match the prototype's mobile heading sizes and line-heights.Medium
G25The phone number isn't visible in the mobile header. The prototype's mobile header shows the tracked number and a "FREE REVIEW →" button. Live shows the logo and a hamburger only, so the number is hidden in the menu.26/26 at 390pxShow the call number (and the review CTA) in the mobile header as in the prototype.High
G26Text is lighter and greyer than the prototype. Headings and body copy use #333 instead of the prototype's ink #111418. Body and lede text is weight 300 instead of 400; eyebrows and card titles are 500 instead of 600.26/26, desktop and mobileUse the prototype's ink colour and font weights.Medium
G27The hero form card differs. The live card has a thick black border, while the prototype uses a soft shadow and no border. The prototype puts "Or call (number)" under the card in dark mono text; live moves it inside the card as faint grey "OR CALL" (the contrast failure in the cross-browser report).26/26Match the prototype's card (shadow, no border) and put the call line back under the card in dark text.Medium
G28The bottom CTA layout differs. The prototype is left-aligned with a two-line headline and a non-italic red "decision". Live centres everything on one line, with "decision" in italic.26/26Match the prototype's alignment; the accent class from G23 removes the italic.Low
G29"Week 1" step tag in mixed case. In the How-it-works tags, "Week 1" is typed in mixed case while its siblings are uppercase ("DAY 0", "BY DEADLINE"). The prototype renders all of them uppercase.17/26Type it as "WEEK 1", or apply the same uppercase style as its siblings.Low
G30GTM is included twice. Page head code has a bare <script async src="…gtm.js?id=GTM-PTLMPVGH"> plus the standard GTM snippet for the same container. In a test the browser fetched the container once and started it once, so no duplicate events were seen.26/26Keep only the standard snippet.Low
G31Dead and overlapping code. (a) Four separate UTM mechanisms: site-head cookies (90 days), a site-footer localStorage copy that also rewrites the URL, page-footer localStorage plus .credolegal.com cookies, and Finsweet queryparam. (b) Validators for elements that don't exist (#phone-number, #dob, #submitBtn, #msgphone), copied from another project (a comment still names softileo.info). (c) Font Awesome CSS loaded with no icons used. (d) A script that swaps "2025" for "2026" in the footer text.26/26Consolidate UTM capture into one script (including fbclid, see G20); remove the dead validators, Font Awesome and the year swap.Low
G32Session recording and chat on a form that collects debt details and PII — to verify. Mouseflow (page code) and Microsoft Clarity (loaded via GTM) both record sessions; the Tidio chat and Optibase A/B testing load on every page. Not yet checked: whether the recorders mask the form inputs (name, phone, email, ZIP, the situation text).26/26Confirm both recorders mask every form field, or drop one; include Tidio and Optibase in the privacy review alongside G9.High (verify)

Shortened meta descriptions — approved 24 Sep

One text per page, used for the meta description, og:description and twitter:description, and written into the prototype first. None carries the NY disclaimer snippet: it cannot fit in 160 characters, and every page already has the attorney-advertising disclaimer in its footer. The texts drop two claims from the prototype descriptions:

  • "Every illegal call is up to $1,000": the FDCPA caps statutory damages at $1,000 per lawsuit (§ 1692k(a)(2)(A)), not per call.
  • "Recent CFPB rule changes" on medical-debt-credit-report-removal: to our knowledge the 2025 CFPB medical-debt reporting rule was vacated by a federal court in July 2025; to confirm.

Where these live as comments

Every row below is also a comment in the LP review hub, so the fixes can be worked and resolved in place. Open any prototype with ?review=1 and the sidebar lists them.

  • The 17 General rows are written as slot-portable comments (slot + scope: 'all') — the widget already supports this. Each is stored once (it appears once in "All pages") and shows on every prototype that declares the slot, in that page's "This page" list. They are anchored to the component each defect belongs to: hero H1 (canonical, duplicate domain, og, titles, imagery), hero lede (meta description), hero eyebrow (missing eyebrow), hero filler line (form endpoint, TCPA, form parity, phones), section headline (heading semantics), disclaimer (schema, NAP/entity), Trustpilot strip (dead widget) and the bottom CTA (GA4, CTA labels).
  • Page-specific rows are ordinary page-local comments on that page's own prototype.
  • The four broken pages have no prototype, so their row is logged as a Critical comment on the one-attorney prototype hero, which is the closest All-States page in the set.

Comment text follows one shape: LP AUDIT 22 Sep · <code/priority> · <scope>, then the defect (with the live URL), then the fix.

Suggested order of work

  1. Rebuild the four payday-content pages — they are taking ad traffic for the wrong intent today.
  2. Canonical + domain decision (G1, G2) — one change, biggest SEO effect.
  3. Confirm lead routing and GA4 (G9, G15) — a broken path here invalidates every other metric.
  4. Entity/address, TCPA consent (G8, G10) — compliance, and it contradicts a decision already made.
  5. Titles and descriptions (G3, G7), heading semantics (G5), then the copy passes and polish.

MajorJavaScript errors
Script error: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'querySelectorAll')24 Sept: multi-step.js is loaded from each page's footer code, but the form doesn't use it (no data-form attributes), so it fails on load.
